`fast_conv_size(a, M)`:
```

`cross_h_size(p, x, M)`: compute h_j =∑ p_{j+i}x_i = coeff of conv(p,x_rev)[n-1:2n-1]
```
x_rev = x[::-1]
P = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
X = np.fft.rfft(x_rev, M)
h_full = np.fft.irfft(P*X, M)
return h_full[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Need p length 2n-1. Good.

`lse_components(x, beta, M_conv, M_grad)`:
```

cmax=float(c.max())
shift=beta*(c-cmax); shift=np.clip(shift, -50, 500)
exp=np.exp(shift); se=exp.sum()
if se<=0: p=np.full(Lc,1.0/Lc); L=cmax
else: p=exp/se; L=cmax+np.log(se)/beta
h=cross_h_size(p,x,M_grad)  # ∑p x
return L,h,p,cmax
```

Thus direct objective:
```
def norm_lse_obj_grad(x, beta, Mconv, Mgrad):
  S=sum(x)
  if S<=... return 1e6, zeros
  y=x/S
  c=conv(y)
  cmax=max(c)
  shift=beta*(c-cmax)
  p=exp(shift)/sum
  L=cmax+logsum(beta)
  h=cross(p,y)
  F=2*n*L
  grad=4*n*(h-L)/S
  # maybe zero small
  return F, grad
```

Let's define helpers in detail:

```
def normalize_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(a.size) / a.size
    return a / S
```

`compute_score_array(a)`:
```
a=normalize_array(a)
n=len(a)
if n==0: return inf
Mconv=next_pow2(2*n-1)
c=fast_conv_size(a,Mconv)
if c.size==0: return inf
M=float(np.max(c))
return float(2.0*n*M)  # since sum=1
```
If a length large, M. If original not normalized, score invariant; using normalized good.

`fast_conv_size` should handle M maybe not int.

`components_normalized(y, beta, Mconv, Mgrad)`: y normalized nonneg sum1.
```
c=fast_conv_size(y,Mconv)
c = np.where(c<0,0,c)
cmax=float(c.max())
shift=beta*(c-cmax)
shift = np.clip(shift, -700.0, 0.0) # if any positive due negative? use -700, 0
exp = np.exp(shift)
se=float(exp.sum())
if se <= 0: p=np.full(len(c),1/len(c)); L=cmax
else: p=exp/se; L=cmax+np.log(se)/beta
h=cross_h_size(p,y,Mgrad)
return L,h
```
For β maybe 0? Not.

`norm_lse_fun(x, beta, mu, Mconv, Mgrad)`:
```
x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)

if S <= 1e-14:
   return 1e3, np.zeros_like(x)

# If beta? L not in gradient? As discussed? Wait for LSE L, derivative ∂L=2h. For sum-constrained F=2nL, gradient =4n h. Yes no L. But if h is derivative of L? Yes.
# However LSE L = cmax + logse/beta, p = exp(-?). Derivative still 2h. Good.

# Clean

```

Let's write helper `softmax_lse_fun` top-level:
```
def softmax_lse_fun(z, beta, Mconv, Mgrad):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
       return 1e6, np.zeros_like(z)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    zc = z - zmax
    zc = np.clip(zc, -40.0, 40.0)
    e = np.exp(zc)
    S = float(e.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
       return 1e6, np.zeros_like(z)
    a = e / S
    n = a.size
    # if a all zero? no
    c = fast_conv_size(a, Mconv)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    shift = beta * (c - cmax)
    shift = np.clip(shift, -700.0, 0.0)
    ex = np.exp(shift)
    se = float(ex.sum())
    if se <= 1e-300:
        p = np.full(c.size, 1.0/c.size)
        L = cmax
    else:
        p = ex / se
        L = cmax + float(np.log(se)) / beta
    h = cross_h_size(p, a, Mgrad)
    F = 2.0 * n * L
    grad = 4.0 * n * a * (h - L)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
       grad = np.zeros_like(z)
    grad -= np.mean(grad)  # zero constant component
    return float(F), grad
```
Check if β is integer; okay.

But component h uses p length L, a. cross_h_size Mgrad must be >=3n-2.

`softmax_from_z` similar but maybe no clip 40? Use 50.

`z_from_a(a, floor=1e-12)`:
```
a=np.maximum(a,0); S=a.sum(); if S<... return zeros? n;
a=(1-floor)*a/S + floor/n
z=np.log(a); z-=z.mean(); return z
```

`lbfgs_soft_chunk(z, beta, maxiter, maxfun, Mconv, Mgrad)`:
```
try:
  res = minimize(softmax_lse_fun, z, args=(beta, Mconv, Mgrad), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True)
  return res.x, float(res.fun), int(res.niter)
except Exception as e:
  return z, 1e6, 0
```

```
def run_soft_schedule(a, budget, label, betas=(10,50,200,800,3200,12800,51200,200000), max_chunk=200):
    t_start=time.monotonic(); t_end=t_start+budget
    a = normalize_array(a)
    n = len(a)
    Mconv = next_pow2(2*n-1); Mgrad=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score_array(a)
    z = z_from_a(a)
    # maybe quick fun
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() >= t_end: break
       # If beta too high for current n? okay
       maxiter_chunk = max(40, min(300, int(8.0 * 10000.0 / max(1000,n))))  # tune
       # For high beta, larger? maybe max(50, min(500, int(4.0 * 20000 / n))).
       while time.monotonic() < t_end - 5:
          remaining = t_end - time.monotonic()
          # reduce chunk if small
          maxiter = max(20, int(min(maxiter_chunk, remaining * 20)))  # rough
          if maxiter <= 20: break
          t0 = time.monotonic()
          z, fun, niter = lbfgs_soft_chunk(z, beta, maxiter, maxiter*20, Mconv, Mgrad)
          dt = time.monotonic() - t0
          a = softmax_from_z(z)
          sc = compute_score_array(a)
          if sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); print
          # if niter < maxiter (converged), break beta
          if niter < maxiter and not (sc < best_sc+1e-9):
             # maybe converged
             break
          # if fun not finite or no time, break
          if not np.isfinite(fun): break
       if best_sc <= 1.503? continue high beta maybe to polish
    # final: also try reverse/average with remaining
    if time.monotonic() < t_end - 30:
       ar = best_a[::-1].copy()
       sc = compute_score_array(ar); if better best...
       avg = 0.5*(best_a+ar); sc...
    return best_a, best_sc
```

    verified=evaluate_sequence(out)
    print
    return out

`lbfgs_chunk` implementation:
```
def lbfgs_chunk(z, beta, maxiter, Mconv, Mgrad):
    try:
       res = minimize(softmax_lse_fun, z, args=(beta, Mconv, Mgrad), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                      options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': max(maxiter*50, 1000), 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':50, 'm':20})
       return res.x, float(res.fun), int(res.niter), int(res.status)
    except Exception:
       return z, 1e20, 0, -1
```
Use `maxfunc` maybe `int(max(1000, maxiter*20))`.

If `res.fun` nan, return.

Now, `interpolate_sequence`:
```
def interpolate_sequence(a, m):
    a=normalize_array(a)
    n=len(a)
    if m==n: return a
    if m<=1: return np.ones(m)
    # if downsample via averaging to preserve sum? Use reshape if divisible else linear interp.
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,m); b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a); b=np.maximum(b,0); S=b.sum(); if S>0: b/=S
    return b
```
For large n to small, interp okay.

Maybe block averaging better:

if m < n and n % m ==0: b=a.reshape(m, n//m).mean(axis=1)
else interp
```

Helper details:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`fast_conv_size`:
```
def fast_conv_size(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)
    return c[:2*a.size-1]
```
If a empty? not.

`cross_h_size`:
```
def cross_h_size(p, a, m):
    n = a.size
    pr = p / max(float(p.sum()), 1e-300) # ensure sum1
    ar = a[::-1]
    P = np.fft.rfft(pr, m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(ar, m)
    h_full = np.fft.irfft(P * A, m)
    return h_full[n-1:2*n-1]
```
p already sum1 but clip.

`lse_parts`:
```
def lse_parts(a, beta, mconv, mgrad):
    c = fast_conv_size(a, mconv)
    if c.size == 0: return np.inf, np.zeros(a.size), 0.0
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if beta <= 1e-9:
       p = np.full(2*a.size-1, 1.0/(2*a.size-1))
       L = float(c.sum() * p.sum())? If beta 0 L=mean c? But beta not 0.
    shift = beta*(c - cmax)
    shift = np.clip(shift, -700.0, 0.0)
    ex = np.exp(shift)
    se = float(ex.sum())
    if se <= 1e-300:
       p = np.full(c.size, 1.0/c.size); L = cmax
    else:
       p = ex / se
       L = cmax + float(np.log(se)) / beta
    h = cross_h_size(p,a,mgrad)
    return L,h,cmax
```
We won't call beta<=. If beta huge, okay. If cmax=0? a not normalized? only uniform? no.

`score_normalized`:
```
def score_normalized(a):
    a=normalize_array(a)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    m=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    c=fast_conv_size(a,m)
    cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(cmax): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*cmax)
```

`normalize_array`:
```
def normalize_array(a):
    if a is None: return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0: return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-14:
       return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    return a/S
```
Maybe clipping to 1000 arbitrary; parent could have >1000 but score invariant; clipping changes. Evaluate also clips to 1000. If parent had values >1000, evaluation clips; internal should mimic? But normalized values likely <1000. If not, we should not clip to 1000 before normalizing? Evaluate clips min(1000) after nonneg, then if all large? If parent values all >1000, clipping to 1000 changes sequence but score invariant to uniform? If all clipped equal? But parent likely normalized. For safety, don't clip to 1000 in normalize; just finite nonneg. Then output clip to 1000 anyway. If values huge, normalize will make <=1 anyway. Use `np.clip(a,0,None)`. If finite negative ->0.
```
a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0); a=np.maximum(a,0)
```

`softmax_from_z`:
```
def softmax_from_z(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size==0: return np.ones(0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): z=np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-50.0)
    zmax=float(z.max())
    zc=z-zmax
    zc=np.clip(zc, -60.0, 0.0) # max 0
    e=np.exp(zc)
    S=float(e.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(z.size)/z.size
    return e/S
```
If z has all -inf? handled.

`z_from_a`:
```
def z_from_a(a, floor=1e-12):
    a=normalize_array(a)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    # if min zero, add small floor without changing much
    amin=float(a.min())
    if amin <= 0.0:
       a = (1.0 - floor)*a + floor/n
    else:
       # still ensure positive if very small? no
       pass
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return z
```
If a has zeros, floor=1e-12/n adds tiny; okay. Maybe floor too small for n=20000 (5e-17) near underflow? log -37, fine. Use 1e-10 maybe to avoid L-BFGS pushing to zero. But adding 1e-10/n changes proportions tiny. Use `floor=1e-12`? For n=16k, 6e-17 below exp clip -60 (8e-27). Actually a_i maybe as low as 1e-20 in optimum? Not necessarily. If we floor to 1e-12/n=6e-17, log -37 above -60, okay. Good. If true optimum has zero, floor not harmful.

`lbfgs_chunk`:
```
def lbfgs_chunk(z, beta, maxiter, mconv, mgrad):
    maxiter = int(max(10, maxiter))
    try:
       res = minimize(softmax_lse_fun, z, args=(beta, mconv, mgrad), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                      options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': max(1000, maxiter*50), 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':50, 'm':20})
       x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
       fun = float(res.fun)
       niter = int(res.niter) if res.niter is not None else 0
       status = int(res.status) if res.status is not None else -2
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)) or not np.isfinite(fun):
           return z, 1e20, 0, -1
       return x, fun, niter, 0
    except Exception:
       return z, 1e20, 0, -1
```
Use `m=10` maybe for memory? 20 okay.

`run_soft_schedule`:

```
def run_soft_schedule(a, budget, beta_list=None, label=""):
    if budget <= 0: return normalize_array(a), score_normalized(a)
    if beta_list is None:
       beta_list = (8, 32, 128, 512, 2048, 8192, 32768, 131072, 500000)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    end = t_start + float(budget)
    a0 = normalize_array(a)
    if a0 is None or a0.size == 0:
       return np.ones(1024), np.inf
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = score_normalized(a0)
    n = a0.size
    mconv = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    mgrad = next_pow2(3*n - 2)
    cur_a = a0.copy()
    cur_z = z_from_a(cur_a)
    # Estimate chunk size
    if n > 12000:
       chunk = 120
    elif n > 8000:
       chunk = 180
    elif n > 4000:
       chunk = 250
    else:
       chunk = 350
    print(f"[{label}] start n={n} score={best_sc:.8f} budget={budget:.0f}s")
    for beta in beta_list:
       if time.monotonic() >= end - 2:
          break
       # Maybe skip very high beta if remaining very small
       if time.monotonic() > end - 60 and beta > 8192:
          break
       while time.monotonic() < end - 4:
          remaining = end - time.monotonic()
          maxiter = chunk
          # If remaining small, reduce
          if remaining < 30:
             maxiter = min(maxiter, 50)
          if maxiter < 20: break
          t0 = time.monotonic()
          cur_z, fun, niter, status = lbfgs_chunk(cur_z, float(beta), maxiter, mconv, mgrad)
          dt = time.monotonic() - t0
          a_new = softmax_from_z(cur_z)
          sc = score_normalized(a_new)
          if np.isfinite(sc):
             cur_a = a_new
             if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                 best_sc = sc
                 best_a = a_new.copy()
                 print(f"[{label}] beta={beta} new best {best_sc:.8f} t={dt:.1f}s")
          if status == 0 and niter < maxiter and sc >= best_sc - 1e-9:
              # likely converged for this beta
              break
          if not np.isfinite(fun):
              break
       # small reset to best if cur bad? If best_sc improved, use best for next? Use cur_a; if best improved, maybe best better than cur? Take best if curvature? After each beta, if score(cur_a) > score(best_a) + 0.001, reset cur_a=best_a, cur_z=z_from_a(best_a). This prevents drift to worse basin.
       if time.monotonic() < end - 10:
          sc_cur = score_normalized(cur_a)
          if not np.isfinite(sc_cur) or sc_cur > best_sc + 1e-4:
              cur_a = best_a.copy(); cur_z = z_from_a(cur_a)
    # Try reverse/average at end if time
    if time.monotonic() < end - 10:
       ar = best_a[::-1].copy()
       sc = score_normalized(ar)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_a = ar.copy(); best_sc = sc
       avg = 0.5*(best_a + best_a[::-1])
       avg = normalize_array(avg)
       sc = score_normalized(avg)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_a = avg; best_sc = sc
    return best_a, best_sc
```

def mirror_polish(a, budget, beta=50000.0, label=""):
    t_start... end
    a0=normalize_array(a); best...
    n... m...
    eta=0.1
    while time < end-3:

       L,h,cmax = lse_parts(a0,beta,mconv,mgrad) # but need current a? use cur_a
       cur_sc = 2*n*cmax
       if cur_sc < best_sc: update
       g=4.0*n*(h-L)
       gnorm=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if not finite or gnorm<1e-12: break
       d = -g/gnorm
       accepted=False
       for frac in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125]:
          e = eta*frac
          if e <= 1e-15: continue
          cand = a0 * np.exp(e*d)
          S=float(cand.sum())
          if S <=0: continue
          cand = cand/S
          sc = score_normalized(cand)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
              best_sc=sc; best_a=cand.copy(); a0=cand.copy(); accepted=True; eta=min(e*1.2,1.0)
              break
       if not accepted:
          eta *= 0.5
    return best_a,best_sc

```
def interpolate_sequence(a, m):
    a=normalize_array(a)
    if a is None: return np.ones(m)
    if m <= 1: return np.ones(m)
    n=a.size
    if m == n: return a
    if m < n and m > 0:
       # if n large and m divides roughly, use interp x
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n); x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m); b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0); S=float(b.sum()); if S>0: b/=S; else b=np.ones(m)
    return b
```
For n=1, x_old length1; np.interp works? If n=1, x_old [0]; x_new all interpolate constant? Use if n==1 return ones(m). Good.

def fallback_initial(n):
    if n<2: return np.ones(n)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    # asymmetric sine/cosine
    a=1.0 + 0.45*np.sin(np.pi*x) + 0.25*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-6); a/=a.sum(); return a

       d = -g/gnorm
       accepted=False
       for frac ...
          cand = cur_a * np.exp(e*d)
          S=...; cand=cand/S
          sc=score_normalized(cand)
          if finite and sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=cand.copy(); cur_a=cand.copy(); eta=min(e*1.25,2.0); accepted=True; break
       it += 1
       if not accepted:
          eta *= 0.5
          if eta < 1e-5: break
   return best_a,best_sc
```

```python
"""
Softmax L-BFGS optimizer for minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
with non-negative heights.

The main idea is to optimize a softmax parameterization
    a_i = exp(z_i) / sum_j exp(z_j)
and use a log-sum-exp approximation to the maximum convolution coefficient.
The LSE approximation is made on the *normalized* sequence, so the smooth
objective is scale invariant.  Several LSE temperature (beta) levels are
used, from smooth to nearly exact.  Chunked L-BFGS calls keep the routine
safe under a time budget.  Final stages add alternative length trials,
small stochastic restarts, mirror-polish, and exact reverse/average
transform checks.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_array(a):
    if a is None:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def fast_conv_size(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)
    return c[:2 * a.size - 1]


def cross_h_size(p, a, m):
    """
    Return h_j = sum_k p_k * a_{k-j} for j=0..n-1,
    computed by FFT as coefficients of conv(p, reverse(a)).
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    ps = float(p.sum())
    if ps <= 1e-300:
        p = np.full(p.size, 1.0 / max(1, p.size))
    else:
        p = p / ps
    ar = a[::-1]
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(ar, m)
    h_full = np.fft.irfft(P * A, m)
    return h_full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def lse_parts(a, beta, mconv, mgrad):
    """
    For normalized non-negative a (sum 1), compute LSE-approximated
    max convolution coefficient L and its gradient ingredients:
        p_k = softmax(beta*(c_k - max(c)))
        h_j = sum_k p_k * a_{k-j}
    The derivative of L wrt a is 2*h.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = fast_conv_size(a, mconv)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())

    if beta <= 1e-9:
        L = float(c.mean())
        p = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / max(1, c.size))
    else:
        shift = beta * (c - cmax)
        shift = np.clip(shift, -700.0, 0.0)
        ex = np.exp(shift)
        se = float(ex.sum())
        if se <= 1e-300:
            p = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / max(1, c.size))
            L = cmax
        else:
            p = ex / se
            L = cmax + float(np.log(se)) / beta

    h = cross_h_size(p, a, mgrad)
    return float(L), h, cmax


def score_normalized(a):
    a = normalize_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    c = fast_conv_size(a, m)
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax)


def softmax_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.ones(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    zc = z - zmax
    zc = np.clip(zc, -60.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(zc)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def z_from_a(a, floor=1e-12):
    a = normalize_array(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if float(a.min()) <= 0.0:
        a = (1.0 - floor) * a + (floor / float(n))
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    z = np.log(a)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return z


def softmax_lse_fun(z, beta, mconv, mgrad):
    """
    Objective and gradient for L-BFGS in the softmax variables.

    f(z) = 2*n*LSE_max(conv(a,a)), a = softmax(z).
    The returned gradient is for the softmax parameterization.
    """
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-50.0)

    a = softmax_from_z(z)
    n = int(a.size)

    L, h, cmax = lse_parts(a, float(beta), mconv, mgrad)
    F = 2.0 * float(n) * L

    # grad f wrt a is 4*n*h.  For softmax:
    # grad f wrt z_i = a_i * (grad_a_i - sum_j a_j grad_a_j)
    # and sum_j a_j h_j = L, hence:
    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * a * (h - L)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros_like(z)
    grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
    return float(F), grad


def lbfgs_chunk(z, beta, maxiter, mconv, mgrad):
    maxiter = int(max(10, int(maxiter)))
    maxfun = int(max(1000, maxiter * 50))
    try:
        res = minimize(
            softmax_lse_fun,
            z,
            args=(float(beta), mconv, mgrad),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "ftol": 1e-15,
                "gtol": 1e-12,
                "maxls": 50,
                "m": 20,
            },
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        fun = float(res.fun)
        niter = int(getattr(res, "niter", 0))
        status = int(getattr(res, "status", -1))
        if (not np.all(np.isfinite(x))) or (not np.isfinite(fun)):
            return z, 1e30, 0, -1
        return x, fun, niter, status
    except Exception:
        return z, 1e30, 0, -1


def run_soft_schedule(a, budget, beta_list=None, label=""):
    """
    Run chunked L-BFGS on a softmax parameterization over a spectrum of
    LSE temperatures.  Returns best normalized sequence and score.
    """
    budget = float(budget)
    if budget <= 1.0:
        a0 = normalize_array(a)
        if a0 is None:
            return np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")
        return a0, score_normalized(a0)

    a0 = normalize_array(a)
    if a0 is None:
        a0 = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)

    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = score_normalized(a0)
    n = int(a0.size)
    mconv = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    mgrad = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    if beta_list is None:
        if np.isfinite(best_sc) and best_sc < 1.70:
            beta_list = (20, 100, 500, 2500, 10000, 50000, 200000, 1000000)
        else:
            beta_list = (5, 20, 100, 500, 2500, 10000, 50000, 200000, 1000000)

    if n > 12000:
        chunk = 110
    elif n > 8000:
        chunk = 160
    elif n > 4000:
        chunk = 230
    else:
        chunk = 320

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    end = t_start + budget

    print(f"[{label}] start n={n} score={best_sc:.8f} budget={budget:.0f}s")

    cur_a = a0.copy()
    cur_z = z_from_a(cur_a)

    for beta in beta_list:
        if time.monotonic() >= end - 2.0:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > end - 45.0 and float(beta) >= 50000.0:
            break

        while time.monotonic() < end - 3.0:
            remaining = end - time.monotonic()
            maxiter = chunk
            if remaining < 25.0:
                maxiter = min(maxiter, 40)
            if maxiter < 15:
                break

            t0 = time.monotonic()
            cur_z, fun, niter, status = lbfgs_chunk(cur_z, float(beta), maxiter, mconv, mgrad)
            dt = time.monotonic() - t0

            a_new = softmax_from_z(cur_z)
            sc = score_normalized(a_new)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                cur_a = a_new
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = a_new.copy()
                    print(f"[{label}] beta={beta:g} new best={best_sc:.8f} "
                          f"chunk_dt={dt:.1f}s")

            if status == 1 and niter < maxiter:
                break
            if not np.isfinite(fun):
                break

        if time.monotonic() >= end - 8.0:
            break

        sc_cur = score_normalized(cur_a)
        if (not np.isfinite(sc_cur)) or (sc_cur > best_sc + 1e-4):
            cur_a = best_a.copy()
            cur_z = z_from_a(cur_a)

    if time.monotonic() < end - 8.0:
        ar = best_a[::-1].copy()
        sc = score_normalized(ar)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_a = ar.copy()
            best_sc = sc
        avg = 0.5 * (best_a + best_a[::-1])
        avg = normalize_array(avg)
        sc = score_normalized(avg)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_a = avg.copy()
            best_sc = sc

    return best_a, best_sc


def mirror_polish(a, budget, beta=50000.0, label="mirror"):
    """
    Safe multiplicative / mirror descent polish using the LSE gradient.
    Always enforces non-negativity and sum=1.
    """
    budget = float(budget)
    if budget <= 5.0:
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    cur_a = normalize_array(a)
    if cur_a is None:
        cur_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)

    best_a = cur_a.copy()
    best_sc = score_normalized(cur_a)
    n = int(cur_a.size)
    mconv = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    mgrad = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    end = t_start + budget
    eta = 0.2
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end - 3.0 and it < 1200:
        L, h, cmax = lse_parts(cur_a, float(beta), mconv, mgrad)
        cur_sc = 2.0 * float(n) * float(cmax)
        if np.isfinite(cur_sc) and cur_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur_sc
            best_a = cur_a.copy()

        g = 4.0 * float(n) * (h - L)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break
        gnorm = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm <= 1e-13:
            break

        d = -g / gnorm
        accepted = False

        for frac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            e = eta * frac
            if e <= 1e-15:
                continue
            cand = cur_a * np.exp(e * d)
            cs = float(cand.sum())
            if cs <= 1e-300:
                continue
            cand = cand / cs
            sc = score_normalized(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = cand.copy()
                cur_a = cand.copy()
                eta = min(e * 1.25, 2.0)
                accepted = True
                break

        it += 1
        if not accepted:
            eta *= 0.5
            if eta < 1e-6:
                break

    return best_a, best_sc


def interpolate_sequence(a, m):
    a = normalize_array(a)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if a is None:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    return b / s


def fallback_initial(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = 1.0 + 0.45 * np.sin(np.pi * x) + 0.25 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    a /= a.sum()
    return a


def rank_lengths(lengths, target):
    pairs = []
    for L in lengths:
        pairs.append((int(abs(int(L) - int(target))), int(L)))
    pairs.sort()
    return [L for _, L in pairs]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search entry point.  Uses the parent from `height_sequence_1` when
    available, otherwise falls back to a smooth asymmetric initializer.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(total_budget) or total_budget <= 120.0:
        total_budget = 2400.0

    hard_end = t0 + total_budget - 360.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
        if h is not None:
            p = normalize_array(h)
            if p is not None and p.size >= 16:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        pass

    if parent is None:
        parent = fallback_initial(8192)
        print("No parent found, using fallback initial sequence.")

    best_a = normalize_array(parent)
    best_sc = score_normalized(best_a)
    print(f"Initial parent: n={best_a.size}, score={best_sc:.8f}")

    # Main workhorse on the best known length.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_end - 120.0:
        rem = hard_end - time.monotonic()
        main_budget = 0.55 * rem
        best_a, best_sc = run_soft_schedule(best_a, main_budget, label="main")
        print(f"After main: n={best_a.size}, score={best_sc:.8f}")

    # Try nearby / alternative length candidates.
    alt_set = set()
    nbest = int(best_a.size)
    alt_candidates = [2048, 4096, 8192, 16384]
    if parent is not None and int(parent.size) not in (2048, 4096, 8192, 16384):
        alt_candidates.append(int(parent.size))
    if nbest < 16384:
        alt_candidates.append(min(16384, max(2048, 2 * nbest)))
    for L in alt_candidates:
        if 2048 <= L <= 16384 and L != nbest:
            alt_set.add(L)

    alt_lengths = rank_lengths(list(alt_set), nbest)[:2]
    for idx, L in enumerate(alt_lengths):
        if time.monotonic() >= hard_end - 150.0:
            break
        allocate = min(0.17 * (hard_end - t0),
                       max(60.0, (hard_end - time.monotonic()) * 0.35))
        a_alt = interpolate_sequence(best_a, L)
        sc_alt = score_normalized(a_alt)
        if sc_alt < best_sc:
            best_a = a_alt.copy()
            best_sc = sc_alt
        # Try only if plausibly competitive, but a wider margin for larger n.
        if sc_alt < best_sc + 0.06:
            a_alt, sc_alt = run_soft_schedule(a_alt, allocate, label=f"alt_{L}_{idx}")
            if sc_alt < best_sc:
                best_a = a_alt.copy()
                best_sc = sc_alt

    # Small stochastic restart from the current best.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_end - 170.0:
        a_pert = best_a * (1.0 + 0.018 * rng.standard_normal(best_a.size))
        a_pert = normalize_array(a_pert)
        sc_pert = score_normalized(a_pert)
        if sc_pert < best_sc + 0.05:
            allocate = min(0.13 * (hard_end - t0),
                           max(60.0, hard_end - time.monotonic() - 150.0))
            if allocate > 50.0:
                a_pert, sc_pert = run_soft_schedule(
                    a_pert,
                    allocate,
                    beta_list=(500, 5000, 50000, 500000),
                    label="perturb",
                )
                if sc_pert < best_sc:
                    best_a = a_pert.copy()
                    best_sc = sc_pert

    # Mirror polish.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_end - 130.0:
        allocate = min(140.0, hard_end - time.monotonic() - 100.0)
        if allocate > 40.0:
            a_m, sc_m = mirror_polish(best_a, allocate, beta=100000.0, label="mirror")
            if sc_m < best_sc:
                best_a = a_m.copy()
                best_sc = sc_m

    # Final high-temperature LSE polish.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_end - 90.0:
        allocate = max(40.0, hard_end - time.monotonic() - 80.0)
        if allocate > 40.0:
            a_f, sc_f = run_soft_schedule(
                best_a,
                allocate,
                beta_list=(10000, 100000, 1000000),
                label="final",
            )
            if sc_f < best_sc:
                best_a = a_f.copy()
                best_sc = sc_f

    # Final exact-style simple transforms.
    for transform_name, cand in [
        ("rev", best_a[::-1].copy()),
        ("avg", 0.5 * (best_a + best_a[::-1])),
    ]:
        cand = normalize_array(cand)
        if cand is None:
            continue
        sc = score_normalized(cand)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_a = cand.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            print(f"Final transform {transform_name}: {best_sc:.8f}")

    out_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(out_arr.sum()) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5 for _ in range(64)]
    else:
        out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_sc

    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal score={best_sc:.8f}, verified={verified:.8f}, len={len(out)}")
    print(f"elapsed={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s / budget={total_budget:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 70)

    return out
```
